Evaluating on five independent seed pairs (ten BERT models) across three benchmark datasets (SST-2, Stanford Politeness, TweetEval Emotion), our full pipeline produces more universal features (Pearson r $\geq$ 0.70 across seeds) than post-hoc alignment baselines on all three datasets. A minimal qualitative analysis confirms that high-universality features encode interpretable sociolinguistic patterns.

</details>


### [37] [When the Judge Changes, So Does the Measurement: Auditing LLM-as-Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08535)
*Zongyou Yang,Yinghan Hou,Xiaokun Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM作为评估者时的“评估者替换歧义”问题：即使候选回答不变，更换评估模型也会导致评分变化。通过对比Qwen3和MiniMax两种升级路径，发现评估者升级不可互换，强评估者能减少但无法消除位置和冗长偏差，陪审团机制在误差相关时效果有限，结构化辩论效果显著但需完整审计日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出LLM作为评估者时，评估模型的更换会导致评分波动，这一“评估者替换歧义”本质上是一个测量效度问题，现有的评估实践缺乏对这一问题的系统认知和处理。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上，对比两种实际可用的升级路径：Qwen3密集模型从1.7B扩展到32B，以及MiniMax M2-M2.7发布的API版本。系统性地分析评估者升级对评分的稳定性和偏差影响。

Result: 评估者升级不可互换；仅Qwen3从1.7B到4B具有稳健的相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本之间无此特性；强评估者能减少但无法消除位置偏差和冗长偏差；重复采样陪审团在误差相关时效果有限；结构化辩论能显著改变判断结果，但缺乏解析器和回退日志时无法归因于辩论本身。

Conclusion: 建议LLM作为评估者的报告应包含数据集切片、偏差探测、误差依赖估计和协议审计轨迹，以提高评估结果的可信度和可解释性。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [38] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO是一种针对MoE语言模型的结构化剪枝框架，通过将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其平稳分布捕捉跨层依赖关系，从而在50%压缩率下比现有方法平均性能保持率提升最高10.61%，且跨任务方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法主要针对密集Transformer设计，使用基于局部启发式的方法评估专家重要性，忽略了MoE路由中专家之间相互依赖的特性，导致剪枝后的模型在内存占用和泛化性能上存在瓶颈。

Method: 将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其平稳分布编码跨层依赖关系，从而得到全局感知的重要性启发式指标，实现与路由机制一致的结构化剪枝。

Result: 在涵盖安全、偏见与伦理等五个不同领域上，在50%压缩率下平均性能保持率比最先进基线方法高出最多10.61%，且跨任务方差显著更低。

Conclusion: 全局、与路由一致的结构化剪枝能够产生跨异构任务更稳定泛化的模型，验证了建模路由依赖关系对MoE剪枝的重要性。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [39] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree是一种无需训练的最佳优先草稿树方法，通过Domino的条件非因子化校正沿每条根到节点路径评分，在Qwen3-4B上实现最高6.6倍加速（平均接受长度达10.7 tokens/轮），并通过GPU原生CUDA图构建器保持低开销，在吞吐量上全面优于DDTree/CaDDTree/Domino/DFlash等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法存在局限：块扩散起草器（如DFlash）仅建模逐位置边缘分布，最佳优先树方法（如DDTree）基于因子化公式无法表示路径依赖结构；而Domino虽引入GRU因果校正使分布路径依赖，但其解码器与树方法不兼容。因此需要一种能结合Domino条件校正优势与高效树结构的方法。

Method: 提出DominoTree，一种无需训练的最佳优先草稿树方法。其核心创新是使用Domino的条件非因子化校正沿每条根到节点路径对候选树进行评分，通过将每节点校正限制在候选top-M来保证实用性。树构建采用GPU原生CUDA图构建器（与Python参考实现比特一致），保持每轮树构建的低成本。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上8个基准测试中达到最高6.6倍加速，所有温度下平均接受长度最高（10.7 tokens/轮）。相比Domino解码器，吞吐量在所有温度下提升9-10%（Alpaca上最高+22%）；相比DDTree/CaDDTree，所有温度下均胜出。在Qwen3-8B上，所有温度下保持最高接受长度，T=0时吞吐量比DDTree高+24%，高温下持平；整体聚合结果优于DFlash和Domino。

Conclusion: DominoTree通过将Domino的条件非因子化校正与最佳优先树结构结合，在无需额外训练的情况下显著提升了推测解码的效率和吞吐量，证明了路径依赖校正与树结构结合的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [40] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UltraX是一个函数调用精炼框架，通过引入插入操作（除删除和修改外）构建完整的编辑函数空间，实现对大规模预训练数据的细粒度实例级编辑。它采用数据集自适应提示优化、行对齐映射和动态上下文替换等机制生成可靠的程序监督，并通过低置信度过滤和比率控制采样提升训练稳定性。实验表明UltraX在所有语料上平均性能最优，且数据效率和精炼可靠性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练数据接近物理极限，Scaling Laws的收益递减，提升LLMs更多依赖高质量数据而非数据扩张。现有精炼方法存在质量、效率和可靠性局限：基于规则的方法受固定启发式约束，难以处理实例级变化；基于LLM的方法提升质量但无法满足大规模数据处理的效率和可靠性需求。

Method: UltraX框架提出函数调用精炼方法，构建包含插入、删除和修改的完整编辑函数空间。具体包括：(1) 数据集自适应提示优化指导专家LLM生成高质量端到端精炼文本；(2) 行对齐映射和动态上下文替换将原始-精炼文本对转换为结构化程序监督；(3) 低置信度示例过滤和按操作组合的比率控制采样提升监督质量并稳定训练分布；(4) 推理阶段采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统后处理提升大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: UltraX在所有语料上取得最高平均性能，并且在使用更少训练token的情况下匹配或超越基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX通过函数调用框架有效解决了大规模预训练数据精炼中的质量、效率和可靠性挑战，为后Scaling Law时代的LLM数据优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [41] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WebSwarm，一种渐进式递归委托框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、耦合局部目标与搜索模式、递归委托与证据聚合，在深度和广度搜索任务上超越单智能体和多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的搜索智能体存在深度与覆盖难以兼顾的问题：单ReAct智能体受限于长轨迹和有限上下文；多智能体系统虽通过并行执行提升覆盖，但在递归深度、协作适应性和证据驱动的扩展方面仍有明显局限。

Method: WebSwarm采用渐进式递归委托框架：（1）动态实例化搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式；（2）节点可自行解决目标或进一步委托子节点，子节点解决后将证据向上返回，供父节点扩展、修正或聚合；（3）先探测Web上任务相关信息的组织形式以指导节点扩展；（4）跨同质子节点复用过程级经验。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA四个基准上，WebSwarm在深度、宽度及深度-宽度交错任务上均持续优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: WebSwarm通过递归委托与动态协作有效平衡了搜索深度与覆盖范围，消融实验、任务难度分析、Web工具效率评估和模型泛化分析进一步验证了其有效性，为多智能体搜索系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>


### [42] [Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08700)
*Ethan Leung,Elias Lumer,Corey Feld,Austin Huber,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在深度研究系统中，使用LLM作为裁判评估引用质量时的校准问题。通过8个LLM裁判在1,248个标准决策上的实验，发现廉价模型也能达到竞争性表现（如GPT-5-mini在来源相关性上F1=0.908），但不同裁判在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上存在显著差异，表明标量F1会掩盖方向性偏差，而这对强化学习训练至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中使用LLM裁判作为奖励模型时，需要了解裁判的能力和偏差。特别是在深度研究系统中，引用质量评估是一个结构化的标准任务，需要验证LLM裁判的校准性，以确保其作为奖励信号的可信度。

Method: 在对抗性长文基准上，对8个来自3个模型家族的现成LLM裁判进行评估，涵盖1,248个标准决策（全部经人工审核，其中378个困难案例由裁判分歧裁决）。评估维度包括来源相关性和事实支持两个引用质量标准。

Result: GPT-5-mini在来源相关性上取得最强通过类F1（0.908，κ=0.636）；事实支持维度上所有裁判统计上无显著差异。但裁判在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上差异显著，标量F1掩盖了这些方向性偏差。

Conclusion: 在使用引用标准作为奖励信号之前，必须对裁判进行校准。校准并不需要最昂贵的模型，但标量F1不足以评估裁判的偏差，需要关注方向性指标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>


### [43] [Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08731)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以葡萄牙国家语言模型AMALIA-9B为例，提出“恢复差距”方法检验模型在道德权威构念编码中的表面相关性捷径，发现分解提示后性能下降约50%，表明模型依赖道德愤怒等表面特征而非理论推理，呼吁主权LLM基准测试不仅评估与人类编码员的一致性，还应检验一致性背后的证据路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家语言模型被用于测量公民言行与价值观，但现有评估仅关注模型与人类编码员的一致性（信度），而非构念测量的效度。对于需要从表面特征推断的理论构念（如道德基础理论中的“权威”），模型可能通过相关捷径而非遵循理论规则达到正确编码，这一问题尚未被系统检验。

Method: 提出“恢复差距”方法：将整体提示分解为编码手册的原子子句，再按理论明确规则重组，比较分解后性能与整体性能之间的损失。若校准能缩小该差距，则表明测量具有一定的跨模型/语言可移植性；否则说明构念-模型仪器是失败的主要根源。实验将校准后的英语测量工具迁移至AMALIA-9B和欧洲葡萄牙语。

Result: 对于所测试的一个构念（权威）和一个语料库，分解仅恢复了AMALIA整体性能的大约一半。错误分析表明模型依赖表面相关性，尤其是权威人物附近的道德愤怒情绪。相反，一个开源多语言LLM在相同指令下对相同葡萄牙语料库缩小了恢复差距，排除了语料库作为主要解释因素。

Conclusion: AMALIA可用于筛选和预编码，但尚不足以独立测量该构念。本研究作为一个反例，呼吁主权LLM基准测试不仅应评估与人类编码员的一致性，还应检验一致性所依据的证据路径。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [44] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench是首个基于能力驱动的基准测试，用于在动态真实世界环境中评估主动智能体，包含400个双语真实世界任务、细粒度的逐步检查点评估以及包含执行器、隐藏监督器和用户代理的闭环评估策略，旨在解耦基础模型能力与框架设计对智能体性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多依赖沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，其基于场景的任务分类将多种模型能力混杂在同一任务类别中，难以定位智能体失败的根因，因此缺乏一个能够精准评估主动智能体基础能力并识别失败原因的基准。

Method: 构建了五个基础模型能力（技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解、跨平台协调）的框架，设计400个双语真实世界任务；在实时Docker容器中通过细粒度逐步检查点评估；设计包含执行器代理、隐藏监督器代理和用户代理的闭环评估策略以模拟多轮人类反馈。

Result: 通过多种模型和框架的全面对比，揭示了基础模型能力和代理框架设计如何共同影响真实环境中的性能表现。

Conclusion: UniClawBench能够有效评估主动智能体在动态真实环境中的能力，并帮助解耦模型能力与框架设计对性能的影响，为未来研究提供基准和代码。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>

### [45] [AI-Driven Thermal Mapping and Management in 3D Integrated Photonic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07711)
*Liton Kumar Biswas,Katayoon Yahyaei,Shajib Ghosh,M Shafkat M Khan,Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Rayhane Ghane-Motlagh,Mahdi Nikdast,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合AI热建模框架与设计启发式方法的双方案解决方案，用于解决三维光子集成电路（3D PIC）中的热管理挑战，通过融合稀疏传感器数据与设计层信息，实现多层温度变化预测和精确热映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维光子集成电路因高密度键合和异质材料引入严重的热管理挑战，而传统热显微镜和封装内传感器等方法仅能提供稀疏数据，无法获取完整的热分布信息。

Method: 采用双方法解决方案：1）AI驱动热建模框架，融合稀疏传感器数据与设计层及密度信息预测多层温度变化；2）设计启发式方法，利用局部材料属性、设计布局、组件几何形状和传感器坐标优化特定区域的热估计。通过插值传感器数据并结合设计区域间比较分析调整局部热阻，生成3D PIC的2D热图。

Result: 启发式方法补充了AI模型，无需大量训练数据即可提升估计精度。两者结合为实时热映射和设计时仿真提供了可扩展、准确的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该双方法方案能够实现下一代3D光子系统中可靠的热管理。

Abstract: Photonic Integrated Circuits (PICs) are advancing high-performance computing, data centers, and sensing, yet three-dimensional (3D) PICs introduce critical thermal management challenges due to high-density bonding and heterogeneous materials. Traditional methods like thermal microscopes and in-package sensors yield sparse data, limiting full thermal profile visibility. This paper presents a dual-method solution combining an AI-driven thermal modeling framework with a design-based heuristic approach. The AI method integrates sparse sensor data with design layer and density information to predict multilayer temperature variations, while the heuristic approach uses localized material properties, design layout, component geometries, and sensor coordinates to refine thermal estimations in specific regions. A 2D thermal map of a 3D PIC is generated by interpolating sensor data and adjusting for local thermal resistivity using comparative analysis between design regions. The heuristic method complements the AI model, improving estimation accuracy without extensive training data. Together, these methods offer a scalable, accurate solution for real-time thermal mapping and design-time simulation, enabling reliable thermal management in next-generation 3D photonic systems.

</details>


### [46] [A Theoretical Framework for Stochastic Activity Prediction in Tensor Accelerator Wallace-Tree Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08002)
*Prashanthi Metku,Chandra Gandu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出随机活动预测（SAP）技术，通过检测操作数汉明重量预测乘法器内部开关活动，并在安全控制器确认重用正确时冻结输入，从而在稀疏张量加速器中节省动态功耗，误预测仅损失节能机会而不会导致计算错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术无法解决张量加速器乘法器在稀疏操作数场景下的动态功耗问题：零检测要求操作数精确为零，结构功耗门控要求乘法器空闲，离线权重选择无法响应运行时数据。需要一种能在乘法器执行前预测并减少非必要动态功耗的方法。

Method: SAP方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 在执行前检测操作数的汉明重量；(2) 预测低开关活动时冻结乘法器输入；(3) 确定性安全控制器独立验证重用正确性。基于三个形式化理论支撑：谱收缩引理（Lipschitz常数Lφ=3/2，256周期窗口预测误差<10^{-13}）、信息保留定理（η_I≥1-O(log n/n)，每周期1比特捕获O(n^2)内部节点的预测信息）、伯努利最优性定理（证明所选编码在标定单比特编码器中最优）。

Result: 理论分析表明谱收缩引理保证预测误差低于10^{-13}（256周期窗口），信息保留定理证明单比特/周期可捕获近乎全部预测信息，伯努利最优性定理确认编码最优性。误预测仅导致节能机会损失，从不产生错误计算结果。

Conclusion: SAP填补了张量加速器功耗优化栈中现有技术无法覆盖的空缺，为稀疏计算场景下的动态功耗降低提供了可行的形式化解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor accelerator multipliers burn dynamic power on every clock cycle, even when sparse operands require very little internal switching. No existing technique addresses this: zero-detection requires exactly-zero operands, structural power gating requires an idle multiplier, and offline weight selection cannot respond to runtime data. This paper introduces Stochastic Activity Prediction (SAP), which closes this gap by examining the Hamming weight of arriving operands before the multiplier executes, predicting low switching activity, and freezing the inputs when a deterministic Safety Controller independently confirms the reuse is correct. Mispredictions cause missed savings, never wrong answers. Three formal results underpin SAP: (i) a Spectral Contraction Lemma proving that Wallace-tree activity depends on operand bit density, not bit position, establishing Lipschitz constant $Lφ= 3/2$ and prediction error below $10^{-13}$ for a 256-cycle window; (ii) an Information Retention Theorem showing $η_I \ge 1 - O(\log n/n)$, so one bit per cycle captures nearly all predictive information about $O(n^2)$ internal nodes; and (iii) a Bernoulli Optimality Theorem proving the chosen encoding is shown to be optimal, within the family of calibrated one-bit encoders of Hamming-weight statistics considered. SAP addresses the specific layer of the tensor accelerator power stack that existing techniques do not cover.

</details>


### [47] [CRIMP: Compact & Reliable DNN Inference on In-Memory Processing via Crossbar-Aligned Compression and Non-ideality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08015)
*Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Shiqing Li,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一训练框架，同时解决基于交叉存储处理的加速器在DNN推理中面临的浮点运算开销、冗余参数占用和器件非理想特性三个问题，通过重用移位单元实现整数-only量化、核组剪枝与交叉剪枝消除数据对齐需求、以及运行时感知的非理想性适配，在CIFAR-10上对VGG-16和ResNet-56分别仅降低2.19%和1.26%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于交叉存储处理的加速器在加速深度神经网络时面临三大障碍：浮点运算与交叉存储不兼容、现有量化方案仍需浮点处理器导致硬件开销；冗余DNN参数占用过多交叉存储、现有剪枝方法需要数据对齐引入额外开销；非理想交叉存储行为（如写入变异）降低模型精度、现有补偿方法开销大。

Method: 1) 重用交叉存储中的位移单元近似乘法缩放因子，避免浮点处理器；2) 提出核组剪枝和交叉剪枝，消除数据对齐所需的硬件单元；3) 采用运行时感知的非理想性适配，从训练阶段利用交叉存储特性缓解器件非理想性影响。

Result: 所提出的量化方案精度下降可忽略，剪枝方法在稀疏性和精度上均优于现有方法。在CIFAR-10上，VGG-16和ResNet-56的精度分别仅下降2.19%和1.26%，且无额外硬件开销。

Conclusion: 将三种优化集成到一个统一学习框架中可降低训练开销并提高精度，为IMP加速器生成纯整数、剪枝且可靠的DNN模型。

Abstract: Crossbar-based In-Memory Processing (IMP) accelerators achieve high-speed, low-power computing for deep neural networks (DNNs), but face three obstacles. First, floating-point (FP) arithmetic is incompatible with crossbars, and existing quantization schemes still require FP processors for scaling factors, incurring hardware overhead. Second, redundant DNN parameters occupy too many crossbars, and current IMP-aware pruning methods require data aligning across crossbars, introducing significant memory and computing overhead. Third, non-ideal crossbar behaviors such as write variations degrade the accuracy of deployed models, and existing compensation methods add substantial overhead. In this paper, we address all three problems within a single training process. We reuse bit-shift units in crossbars to approximately multiply scaling factors, avoiding FP processors. We apply kernel-group pruning and crossbar pruning to remove the hardware units needed for data aligning. We adopt runtime-aware non-ideality adaptation to relieve the impact of device non-ideality from the training stage by exploiting crossbar features. Integrating these three optimizations into one comprehensive learning framework reduces training overhead and improves accuracy. Experiments show that our quantization incurs a negligible accuracy drop, and our pruning achieves higher sparsity and accuracy than state-of-the-art methods. Our framework produces integer-only, pruned, and reliable VGG-16 and ResNet-56 models for CIFAR-10 on IMP accelerators, with accuracy drops of only 2.19% and 1.26%, respectively, without hardware overhead.

</details>


### [48] [Who Needs DRAM? We Have Fiber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08407)
*Hannah Atmer,Thiemo Voigt,Yuan Yao,Stefanos Kaxiras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出名为"光纤存储器"（Fiber Memory）的新型架构，将光纤重新设计为主动循环延迟线存储器，用于存储不可变数据（如大语言模型权重）。通过空间分割复用多芯光纤、无源光分接放大接口、共封装光学和全光再生技术，该架构能在10000个AI加速器间消除冗余权重存储，相比传统HBM3e配置降低70%以上的权重传输能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI对高性能存储的需求急剧上升，加上超大规模数据中心的扩张消耗了大量DRAM产能，导致DRAM供应紧张和合同价格上涨。现有存储架构难以满足大规模AI模型权重的低成本、高能效分发需求。

Method: 提出数据并行的光广播延迟线存储架构：（1）利用多芯光纤（MCF）实现空间分割复用；（2）设计无源光分接放大接口实现广播分发；（3）采用共封装光学（CPO）减少光电器件距离；（4）引入区域全光再生保证信号质量。光纤作为主动循环存储介质，而非传统的数据传输通道。

Result: 在包含10000个AI加速器的场景中，Fiber Memory可消除冗余的权重存储，并将权重传输能耗降低超过70%（对比传统HBM3e配置）。

Conclusion: Fiber Memory通过重新利用光纤的物理特性，为超大规模数据中心中不可变数据的存储和分发提供了一种高能效、低成本的替代方案，有望缓解DRAM供应压力。

Abstract: The rising pressure on DRAM availability and contract pricing reflects generative AI's massive high-performance memory requirements. This pressure is heavily compounded by hyperscale data center expansion, which now consumes a significant portion of global DRAM output. In this work, we propose a new architecture: Fiber Memory, which reimagines the role of optical fiber in a hyperscale data center, deploying it as an active, recirculating delay-line memory for immutable data, such as large language model (LLM) weights. We present a data-parallel optical broadcast delay-line memory architecture that accounts for fiber's physical realities. By incorporating space-division multiplexed multi-core fibers (MCFs), passive optical tap-and-amplify interfaces, co-packaged optics (CPO), and regional all-optical regeneration, our case study evaluation demonstrates that Fiber Memory can eliminate redundant weight storage across 10,000 AI accelerators and reduce weight-delivery energy by over 70% compared to traditional HBM3e configurations.

</details>


### [49] [FPGN: Redefining Ultra-Fast Programmable Gate-based Neural Acceleration with Differentiable LUTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08427)
*Jiawei Liang,Haotong Qin,Linfeng Du,Xingyu Liu,Shangkun Li,Hui Yu,Michele Magno,Xinyu Chen,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出FPGN，一种端到端的物理感知框架，通过硬件对齐的可微公式、结构化LUT原生拓扑和延迟驱动编译器，弥合了LUT原生学习与延迟优化的FPGA实现之间的差距，相比代表性FPGA加速器实现最高205倍延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LUT原生神经网络方法停留在算法优化层面，未能将理论潜力转化为高性能FPGA加速器：其可微公式与FPGA LUT原语不匹配、物理不感知的拓扑损害可布线性与时序收敛、缺乏自动化优化流程阻碍系统化设计空间探索和高效硬件实现。

Method: 提出FPGN框架：(i) 硬件对齐的可微公式用于训练FPGA原生LUT神经元；(ii) 结构化LUT原生拓扑配合流式硬件架构以改善路由局部性和时序收敛；(iii) 延迟驱动的编译器利用高保真分析质量结果模型自动化设计空间探索和硬件生成。

Result: 相比代表性FPGA二值神经网络加速器实现最高205倍延迟降低，相比先前可微LUT原生网络实现最高30倍LUT效率提升，同时保持具有竞争力的推理精度。

Conclusion: FPGN成功弥合了LUT原生学习与延迟优化的FPGA实现之间的差距，证明了物理感知的协同设计方法对于实现纳秒级推理延迟的关键价值。

Abstract: Achieving nanosecond-scale inference latency for deep neural networks (DNNs) has become a primary architectural concern for latency-critical applications. While Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) offer a promising substrate for low-latency inference, conventional FPGA accelerators remain arithmetic-centric, using LUTs primarily as building blocks for numerical operators and peripheral logic. In contrast, recent LUT-native neural networks treat LUTs as learnable neurons, revealing promising theoretical potential to exploit their intrinsic logic expressivity. However, existing methods are largely confined to algorithmic optimizations, failing to translate this theoretical potential into high-performance FPGA accelerators. Specifically, their differentiable formulations do not faithfully match FPGA LUT primitives, their physically-unaware topologies compromise routability and timing closure, and their lack of automated optimization flow hinders systematic design space exploration (DSE) and efficient hardware implementation.
  In this paper, we propose FPGN, an end-to-end physically-aware framework that closes the gap between LUT-native learning and latency-optimized FPGA implementation. FPGN addresses these challenges through (i) a hardware-aligned differentiable formulation for training FPGA-native LUT neurons, (ii) a structured LUT-native topology with a streaming hardware architecture to improve routing locality and timing closure, and (iii) a latency-driven compiler that leverages high-fidelity analytical Quality of Results models to automate DSE and hardware generation. Experiments show that FPGN achieves up to 205$\times$ latency reduction compared to representative FPGA-based BNN accelerators and up to 30$\times$ higher LUT efficiency than prior differentiable LUT-native networks, while maintaining competitive inference accuracy.

</details>

### [50] [CTA-Pipelining: A Latency-Oriented Spatial Scaling Method for Multi-GPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07862)
*Tingkai Liu,Muralidhar Andoorveedu,Sanjoy Das,Sanjay Patel,Volodymyr Kindratenko*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CTA-pipelining，一种面向共享内存多GPU系统的延迟优化执行范式，通过利用线程块级别的依赖关系实现跨GPU的并行执行，在8-GPU H200和B200系统上相比微批处理和Tensor Parallelism分别降低延迟最高31.8%和29.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多GPU系统已发展为紧密集成的共享内存结构，但软件仍将其视为高速网络；同时LLM服务对延迟敏感的需求使得GPU工作负载优化从吞吐驱动转向延迟约束，现有Tensor Parallelism等扩展方式在延迟优化上存在不足。

Method: 提出CTA-pipelining执行范式，在Cooperative Thread Array（CTA）级别建立依赖关系，使依赖核函数能够在多个GPU上并发执行，利用共享内存多GPU系统的低延迟互连特性实现延迟导向的空间扩展。

Result: 在8-GPU H200和B200系统上，针对2层GEMM（代表MLP操作），CTA-pipelining相比微批处理降低延迟31.8%，相比Tensor Parallelism降低29.6%，且可与TP正交组合进一步优化延迟。

Conclusion: CTA-pipelining是一种有效的延迟导向多GPU扩展技术，通过细粒度的核间依赖调度充分利用共享内存架构优势，可突破现有TP方案的延迟瓶颈。

Abstract: The evolution of compute infrastructure has transformed multi-GPU systems into tightly integrated shared-memory structures. However, current software still mostly treats these coherent interconnects simply as high-speed networks. Simultaneously, the demand for serving Large Language Models under latency constraints has shifted GPU workload optimization from being throughput-driven to latency-bound, necessitating latency-oriented scaling methods beyond Tensor Parallelism (TP).
  Thus, we introduce CTA-pipelining, an execution paradigm designed to exploit shared-memory multi-GPU systems. As a latency-oriented spatial scaling technique, CTA-pipelining leverages dependencies at the Cooperative Thread Array level, enabling concurrent execution of dependent kernels across GPUs. We demonstrate its capability using CUTLASS, cuBLAS, and NCCL libraries on 8-GPU H200 and B200 systems. Results show on 2-layer GEMM, representing the MLP operation, CTA-pipelining reduces latency by up to 31.8% compared to micro-batching, and 29.6% compared to TP. It can also be combined with TP as an orthogonal scaling dimension to further push the latency boundary.

</details>


### [51] [Toward a Unified GPU-Aware OpenSHMEM Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08006)
*Naveen Ravi,Nathan Wichmann,Md. Wasi-ur- Rahman,Aurelien Bouteiller,Yıltan Hassan Temuçin,Avinash Kethineedi,Johnathan Alsop,Brandon Potter,Shubhendra Pal Singhal,Jun Shirako,Akihiro Hayashi,Vivek Sarkar,Lawrence C. Stewart,Michael Beebe,Benjamin Michalowicz,Jeongnim Kim,Thiago Teixeria,Mark F. Brown,Aaron Welch,Oscar Hernandez,Wendy Poole,Steve Poole*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"OpenSHMEM GPU-Aware Communication Auxiliary Specification"的轻量级、向后兼容的扩展规范，旨在为OpenSHMEM 1.x添加GPU感知通信的便携语义，通过引入GPU作用域内存空间抽象、能力查询和明确定义的语义，实现跨多厂商GPU的统一编程模型，防止社区分裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM实现在内存管理、能力发现和操作语义上存在差异，缺乏可移植的加速器架构语义，限制了可移植性和生态系统凝聚力，可能导致OpenSHMEM原本要统一的社区分裂。

Method: 提出了OpenSHMEM辅助规范，作为OpenSHMEM 1.x的轻量级向后兼容扩展。通过引入GPU作用域内存空间抽象的最小内存模型扩展，提供能力查询和明确定义的语义，使GPU附加缓冲区可用于RMA、原子操作、同步和集合操作。初始设计为主机发起接口，但也支持可选的设备发起。

Result: 证明了GPU感知的OpenSHMEM语义可以在多厂商GPU上指定和实现，提供了向厂商无关规范统一的可实践且快速实现的步骤。

Conclusion: 该辅助规范为未来OpenSHMEM规范的设计提供了参考，是统一GPU感知通信生态系统的实用第一步。

Abstract: Leadership-class HPC systems are now accelerator-centric, with GPUs providing most floating-point throughput and memory bandwidth. As next-generation systems increasingly integrate accelerators through high-speed memory fabrics and system interconnects, exposing larger tightly coupled device domains, \ac{PGAS} models such as OpenSHMEM provide a natural abstraction for expressing fine-grained remote memory operations across these devices. While OpenSHMEM 1.x offers a lean PGAS model for irregular communication, atomics, fine-grained synchronization, and collectives, its memory model lacks portable semantics for accelerator architectures. As a result, existing GPU-enabled OpenSHMEM implementations differ in memory management, capability discovery, and operation semantics, limiting portability and ecosystem cohesion. This risks fracturing the community that OpenSHMEM was originally created to unify.
  This paper proposes an OpenSHMEM Auxiliary Specification for GPU-Aware Communication, designed as a lightweight, backward-compatible extension to OpenSHMEM 1.x. The auxiliary specification introduces a minimal memory model extension via a GPU-scoped memory space abstraction, along with capability queries and well-defined semantics for using \acs{GPU}-attached buffers in RMA, atomic, synchronization, and collective operations. This is initially conceived through the lens of a host-initiated interface, although it provides a general set of semantics that also allow for optional device-initiated support. A central goal of this effort is to demonstrate that GPU-aware OpenSHMEM semantics can be specified and implemented across GPUs from multiple vendors, providing a practical and rapidly implementable step toward unification under a vendor-neutral specification while informing the design of future OpenSHMEM specifications.

</details>


### [52] [On the Limitations of Non-GPU AI Accelerators for Large-Model Inference: A Field Study of MoE and Multimodal Serving on Huawei Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08215)
*Zheng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过在大规模模型推理场景中使用华为Ascend 910系统部署两个工作负载（LLM安全评估和医疗视觉语言基准测试），系统记录了非GPU AI加速器（Ascend）在工程迁移中的实际成本，总结了八类平台限制，并提供了可复现的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非GPU AI加速器正被广泛采用，但将大规模模型推理工作负载从CUDA迁移到这些平台的真实工程成本缺乏系统记录。

Method: 在两个工作负载（基于W8A8 MoE模型的LLM安全评估、基于DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision的多模态医学视觉语言基准测试）上，使用CANN和vLLM-Ascend部署在16设备华为Ascend 910系统上，通过源码级补丁、功能禁用和运维保障措施实现可靠运行，并总结平台限制。

Result: 需要12个源码级补丁修复供应商推理插件，禁用多个高吞吐功能以保数值正确性，增加运维保障应对设备级故障；总结出八类平台限制（算子支持不完整、并行脆弱性、数值错误、图编译不成熟、高级功能不稳定、扩展性有限、可观测性弱、生态碎片化），并量化了集成工作量、并发行为和基准质量，证明两个负载正确运行。

Conclusion: 本研究为团队评估或运营非GPU加速器进行大规模模型推理提供了可复现的工程参考。

Abstract: Non-GPU AI accelerators are increasingly adopted as alternatives to general-purpose GPUs for large-model inference, but the real engineering cost of migrating demanding workloads beyond CUDA remains poorly documented. We present a field study of deploying two large inference workloads on a 16-device Huawei Ascend 910 system using CANN and vLLM-Ascend: an LLM-as-a-judge safety and alignment evaluation pipeline based on a W8A8 MoE judge model, DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and a multimodal medical vision--language benchmark based on DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision for MMMU and MMMU-Pro. Making these workloads reliable required twelve source-level patches to the vendor inference plugin, disabling several high-throughput features to preserve numerical correctness, and adding operational safeguards for recurring device-level failures. We summarize the main platform limitations in eight categories: incomplete operator and feature support, fragile parallelism, numerical faults in low-level kernels, immature graph compilation, unstable advanced features, limited scalability, weak observability, and ecosystem fragmentation. For each category, we report the symptoms, evidence, and likely causes. We also quantify the integration effort, concurrency behavior, and benchmark quality to show that both workloads were served correctly. Our study provides a reproducible reference for teams evaluating or operating non-GPU accelerators for large-model inference.

</details>


### [53] [Self-Stabilizing Algorithms in the Uniform Port Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08244)
*Liam Brinker,Yuval Emek,Oren Louidor*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"统一端口模型"的分布式计算模型，其中每个图的端口（半边缘）都托管一个恒定大小的有限自动机，使模型真正统一。该模型支持（半）边缘的输入/输出标签分配，能够自然表述最大匹配和无环定向等（半）边缘标签问题。主要技术贡献是设计了在一般图上针对各种局部对称破缺问题（包括最大独立集、最大匹配、无环定向和最大节点/边k着色）的高效（多对数运行时）自稳定统一端口算法，这是在真正统一模型中的首次展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的节点中心真正统一的分布式计算模型无法表达（半）边缘标签问题（如最大匹配和无环定向），因此需要一种新的计算模型来自然支持这些问题的表述。

Method: 提出了统一端口模型，其中每个端口（半边缘）托管一个恒定大小的有限自动机；基于此模型设计了高效的自稳定算法来解决局部对称破缺问题。

Result: 在一般图上设计了具有多对数运行时的自稳定统一端口算法，用于最大独立集、最大匹配、无环定向和最大节点/边k着色等局部对称破缺问题。

Conclusion: 首次在真正统一的分布式计算模型中证明了高效自稳定算法对于局部对称破缺问题的存在性，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: We introduce a distributed computational model referred to as the \emph{uniform port} model. An algorithm operating in this model is defined by means of local automata associated with the ports (a.k.a.\ half-edges) of the input graph. The crux of the uniform port model is that a single constant-size finite automaton is hosted by every port of every graph, making the model \emph{truly uniform}. Moreover, since the new model explicitly supports the assignment of (input and) output labels to the graph's (half-)edges, it facilitates natural formulations of (half-)edge-labeling problems such as maximal matching and sinkless orientation, which are outside the expressivity scope of prior node-centric truly uniform distributed computational models.
  The main technical contribution of this paper is the design of efficient (i.e., with poly-logarithmic runtime) \emph{self-stabilizing} uniform port algorithms, operating on general graphs, for various fundamental local symmetry breaking problems, including maximal independent set, maximal matching, sinkless orientation, and maximal node/edge $k$-coloring. While efficient self-stabilizing algorithms for local symmetry breaking problems have been extensively studied in stronger computational models, our work is the first to demonstrate the existence of such algorithms in a truly uniform model.

</details>


### [54] [Empirical Analysis of GPU Frequency Behavior Under ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08307)
*Truong-Thanh Le,Hoang-Loc La,Amir Taherkordi,Frank Eliassen,Phuong Hoai Ha,Peiyuan Guan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了NVIDIA GPU在执行ML/AI工作负载时的频率缩放行为，发现低性能GPU的工作频率受最近80ms窗口内工作负载历史的强烈影响，导致GPU内核延迟之间存在依赖关系，挑战了现有延迟预测技术中假设内核延迟相互独立的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ML延迟预测技术假设GPU内核延迟相互独立，通过累加单次测量值估算总执行时间。本研究旨在验证这一假设在实际GPU动态频率缩放行为下是否成立。

Method: 通过实验观察NVIDIA GPU在ML/AI工作负载下的实际运行频率，分析工作负载历史对当前频率的影响，并评估内核延迟之间的依赖关系。

Result: 低性能GPU的工作频率受近80ms窗口内的工作负载历史强烈影响；动态频率缩放导致GPU内核延迟之间存在非独立依赖关系。

Conclusion: 现有延迟预测技术中关于内核延迟独立的假设并不总是成立；未来可在改进延迟预测模型、GPU内核重排序策略以及NAS驱动的频率/延迟/能耗感知模型设计等方向展开研究。

Abstract: This work presents ongoing research on the frequency scaling behavior of NVIDIA GPUs when executing ML/AI workloads. Our preliminary findings show that, on lower-performance GPUs, the operating frequency is strongly affected by the recent workload history, typically within an 80ms window. This behavior challenges a common assumption underlying several state-of-the-art ML latency-prediction techniques, which treat individual GPU kernel latencies as independent and therefore estimate total execution time by summing isolated per-kernel measurements. Our results indicate that such an assumption does not always hold, as the GPU's dynamic frequency scaling introduces inter-kernel dependencies. We also outline several promising directions for leveraging this observation in future work, including improved latency-prediction models, GPU kernel-reordering strategies, and NAS-driven guidelines for frequency/latency/energy-aware model design.

</details>


### [55] [Adaptive Row Selection Meets Asynchrony in Randomized Kaczmarz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08313)
*Evan Coleman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了异步执行环境下随机Kaczmarz方法的自适应行选择策略，通过在96核节点上的339次运行实验，发现了四个关键结论：采样激进性与线程数之间存在稳定边界、阈值贪心选择在高线程数下不稳定、欠松弛可以恢复稳定性、一致快照读存在罕见的调度相关发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机Kaczmarz方法适用于大规模稀疏最小二乘和层析重建问题，自适应行选择可减少迭代次数，但在共享内存机器上部署自适应选择时，无锁工作者会并发修改残差并使用过时数据。目前缺乏对该场景下异步执行行为的系统研究。

Method: 在96核节点上进行339次运行实验，测量残差加权和贪心Kaczmarz在异步执行下的表现，使用实际（而非注入的）延迟，系统研究采样激进性与线程数之间的关系。

Result: (i) 稳定性由边界ℓ*(T)控制；(ii) 阈值贪心选择在高线程数下不稳定，几乎立即发散；(iii) 欠松弛可以在可预测的代价下恢复稳定性；(iv) 一致快照读存在罕见的调度相关发散，而实时（不一致）读从未出现此问题且成本更低。

Conclusion: 不一致读是更好的默认选择；研究结果为分布式两层采样器的设计提供了依据。

Abstract: Randomized Kaczmarz is a natural fit for large sparse least-squares and tomographic reconstruction, and adaptive row selection can reduce iteration counts. However, deploying adaptive selection on a shared-memory machine means sampling from a residual that lock-free workers are concurrently modifying, often using stale data. We present the first systematic study of this regime: residual-weighted and greedy Kaczmarz under asynchronous execution, measured across 339 runs on a 96-core node with realized (not injected) delays. Four findings carry directly to practice. (i) Stability is governed by a boundary $\ell^*(T)$ between sampling aggressiveness and thread count; below it, more aggressive sampling is strictly better, so one should tune to just inside the cliff. (ii) Threshold-greedy selection (the standard accelerated rule) is unstable at high thread counts, diverging almost immediately. (iii) Under-relaxation buys back the cliff at a predictable cost, giving a usable safety knob. (iv) Consistent-snapshot reads admit a rare, scheduling-dependent divergence that live (inconsistent) reads never exhibited and that is also cheaper, making inconsistent reads the right default. We validate the implementation against published sequential results and outline the distributed two-level sampler these measurements motivate.

</details>


### [56] [Computing in Anonymous Dynamic Networks with One-Bit Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08358)
*Thibaut Blanc,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Giovanni Viglietta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了匿名动态网络中确定性计算的下界与上界，证明即使在每轮仅广播1比特、仅知邻居发送比特数的极端限制下，仍可实现任意可计算函数，且复杂度与每消息O(log n)比特的拥塞模型基本匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在匿名动态网络中，当通信能力被极度压缩（每节点每轮仅广播1比特，仅知各比特值的邻居数量）时，全局确定性计算的能力与极限。

Method: (1) 从局部单比特聚合观察中提取全局线性方程组；(2) 设计单比特割测试，获得不可区分代理类大小的守恒约束；(3) 通过细化类并收集独立约束恢复多重集；(4) 针对未知网络规模，提出自纠正自适应洪泛原语。

Result: (1) 已知上界U时，O(n³ log² n + U)轮终止算法；(2) 未知大小时，O(n³ log² n)轮自稳定算法；(3) 几乎匹配的下界Ω(n² log(N/n)/log n)，当N=2^{Ω(n log n)}时变为Ω(n³)；(4) 下界在唯一领导、已知n和N、动态环图下仍成立。

Conclusion: 即使在每消息压缩至1比特的极端限制下，拥塞模型匿名动态网络的计算能力基本得以保留。

Abstract: We initiate the study of deterministic computation in anonymous dynamic networks where each agent broadcasts one bit per round and receives only the number of neighbors broadcasting each bit value. Despite this severe restriction, surprisingly rich global computation is possible.
  With a unique leader and a known upper bound $U$ on the network size $n$, we give a terminating algorithm for any computable function of the input multiset in $O(n^3\log^2 n+U)$ rounds, for inputs from a universe of size $N=2^{O(n\log n)}$. Without prior knowledge of $n$, we design a stabilizing algorithm for the same task running in $O(n^3\log^2 n)$ rounds. This essentially matches the state of the art for the congested model, where messages carry $O(\log n)$ bits and general computation takes $O(n^3)$ rounds. We also obtain comparable results for leaderless and multi-leader networks.
  We complement the upper bounds with an almost-matching lower bound of $$Ω!\left(\frac{n^2\log(N/n)}{\log n}\right)$$ rounds, which becomes $Ω(n^3)$ for $N=2^{Ω(n\log n)}$. The proof is information-theoretic, based on local histories, and holds even with a unique leader, known $n$ and $N$, and a communication graph restricted to a dynamically changing ring.
  Our algorithms extract global linear equations from local one-bit aggregate observations. A one-bit cut test yields conservation constraints on the sizes of indistinguishable agent classes; by refining these classes and collecting independent constraints, agents recover the required multiplicities. For unknown size, we introduce a self-correcting adaptive flooding primitive of independent interest. Thus, the computational power of congested anonymous dynamic networks is essentially preserved even when every message is compressed to one bit.

</details>


### [57] [Coded Task Offloading for Fluid Computing: A Privacy-Aware Approach under D2D Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08440)
*Diego Cajaraville-Aboy,Manuel Fernández-Veiga,Ana Fernández-Vilas,Rebeca P. Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向D2D网络的编码任务卸载方案，结合线性秘密共享对任务进行编码分片，在随机任务到达和队列动态下联合优化时延、能耗与隐私泄露，实验表明该方案优于传统卸载方法，并揭示了时延-能耗-隐私三者间的内在权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务卸载方案很少考虑对抗性执行环境下的隐私风险和信息泄露问题；同时，大多数编码计算方案仅关注拖后腿节点缓解，而未考虑能量感知等系统级目标。

Method: 将任务卸载技术与线性秘密共享方案结合，任务被编码为冗余分片以支持阈值恢复、拖后腿缓解和隐私保护；构建联合优化时延与能耗、并对理论隐私泄露进行惩罚的隐私感知卸载问题；采用分支定界求解器与轻量级启发式调度器求解。

Result: 编码卸载在时延-能耗权衡上优于经典的全卸载和并行卸载方案；启发式调度器达到近最优性能，超越基线方法和现有求解器；隐私泄露惩罚会重塑卸载决策，暴露出时延-能耗-隐私之间的内在权衡。

Conclusion: 所提编码任务卸载方案在D2D网络中能有效兼顾时延、能耗与隐私保护，并揭示了三者间的权衡关系，为隐私敏感的分布式计算提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Fluid Computing aims to support distributed applications execution across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and device resources, motivating task execution mechanisms that adapt to dynamic and privacy-sensitive environments under runtime conditions. In this context, current task offloading schemes rarely address privacy risks and information leakage under adversarial execution settings; furthermore, most coded computing proposals focus on straggler mitigation without considering system-level objectives such as energy awareness. This paper proposes a coded task offloading scheme for D2D networks under stochastic task arrivals and queue-based dynamics. The proposal combines task offloading techniques with linear secret sharing schemes, where tasks are encoded into redundant shares to support threshold-based recovery, straggler mitigation, and privacy preservation while enhancing system performance. Then, we formulate a privacy-aware offloading problem that jointly optimizes delay and energy while penalizing the theoretical privacy leakage of coded tasks under noisy leakage observations. The problem is solved using a branch-and-bound solver alongside a lightweight heuristic scheduler, both of which are evaluated through a discrete-event simulator. Results show that coded offloading improves the delay--energy trade-off with respect to classical full and parallel offloading schemes, while the heuristic achieves near-optimal performance, outperforming baseline and state-of-the-art solvers. The results also show how privacy leakage penalties reshape offloading decisions, exposing an inherent delay--energy--privacy trade-off.

</details>


### [58] [SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08565)
*Jiahao Wang,Kaizhan Lin,Kaixi Zhang,Jinbo Han,Xingda Wei,Sijie Shen,Chenguang Fang,Wenyuan Yu,Rong Chen,Haibo Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本论文针对代理（Agent）场景下的LLM请求调度问题展开研究。现有调度器为了最大化KV缓存复用，过度将请求路由到缓存命中实例，导致实例负载不均，限制了集群吞吐（TPS）。论文提出SMETRIC调度器，通过均衡会话粒度调度：将每个代理会话的首个请求纯用于负载均衡，后续请求再考虑缓存感知路由，从而在保持高缓存复用的同时实现负载均衡。在真实生产轨迹上，SMETRIC在预填-解码共部署场景下提升集群TPS 10-16%，在分离式部署下提升预填TPS 2-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM调度研究主要面向人类对话场景，而代理（Agent）场景下的请求调度特性不同：代理仅在获得完整响应后才行动，因此更关注集群吞吐（TPS）而非单令牌延迟；同时代理请求间存在大量KV缓存复用（生产轨迹中达80%以上）。当前调度器为最大化缓存复用而过度集中路由，导致负载失衡，限制了TPS提升。亟需设计适应代理场景特性的调度策略。

Method: SMETRIC采用均衡会话级调度（Balanced Session-Centric Scheduling）：（1）利用全局KV存储实现跨实例缓存共享，使得负载均衡不必完全牺牲KV复用；（2）利用代理场景中会话内请求的局部性，仅将每个会话的首个请求纯用于负载均衡路由，后续请求则进行缓存感知路由，从而在保持本地实例高缓存复用的同时实现集群负载均衡。调度指标使用会话轮次信息（从用户输入高效获取），使调度器保持无状态。

Result: 在BAILIAN生产轨迹上，SMETRIC在预填-解码共部署场景下相比现有最优调度器提升集群TPS 10-16%；在预填-解码分离部署场景下提升预填TPS 2-34%，同时改善了每令牌延迟。

Conclusion: SMETRIC通过均衡会话级调度策略，在代理场景下有效平衡了KV缓存复用与负载均衡之间的矛盾，显著提升了集群吞吐，验证了"负载均衡不必完全牺牲缓存复用"以及"仅均衡会话首个请求即可"两个关键洞察的有效性。

Abstract: LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat.
  This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances.
  SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.

</details>

### [59] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Context Graph框架实现主动式AI代理，通过构建企业实体关系图、状态变化检测、主动性评分和LLM驱动的推送层，将信息发现时间从反应式的47分钟缩短至30秒以下，Precision@5达0.83。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和智能体系统本质上是被动响应的——等待用户查询后才行动，难以实现真正的企业生产力提升。企业需要能够主动向工作者推送相关、可操作信息的系统。

Method: 提出Context Graph（实时关系数据结构建模企业实体及其关系与状态变迁）、Delta Detection Engine（持续监控状态变化）、Proactivity Scorer（根据紧急性、相关性和角色匹配度排序候选洞察）、以及LLM驱动的Surfacing Layer（推送带可解释性说明的排名通知）。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道维护三个企业用例中，Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率仅0.11，平均信息呈现时间从47分钟（反应式基线）降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图的主动式框架能显著提升企业AI系统的效率，将被动等待转变为主动推送，大幅缩短关键信息的传达时间。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>


### [60] [AI-integrated models for assessing agricultural resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07759)
*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业供应链容易受到生物物理和经济系统耦合带来的 disruptions 影响，现有方法难以跨学科综合评估这些冲击。

Method: 开发AI驱动的工具，集成GTAP经济模型与APSIM生物物理模型，支持自然语言查询与响应。

Result: 实现了跨学科供应链冲击分析，用户可通过自然语言交互获取评估结果。

Conclusion: 该AI工具能够有效整合经济与生物物理模型，为农业供应链的韧性评估和政策制定提供跨学科决策支持。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [61] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"对抗性社会认识论"（ASE），用于分析密集交互的传播环境中，行动者如何利用信任的承诺与权利来扭曲、伪造或策略性模糊信息，并超出传统"认知气泡""回音室"或" misinformation"等概念的描述范畴。文章提供了分析这类现象的语言、破坏信任的机制，以及基于推理主义语义学和认知网络来审计和补救信任违规的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论（认知气泡、回音室、错误信息扩散）不足以解释在多层证词、推理、制度认证和隐性信任构建的传播环境中，行动者如何利用本应使断言可信的承诺与权利来系统性扭曲信息。

Method: 通过构建对抗性社会认识论（ASE），结合推理主义语义学（inferentialist semantics）和认知网络（epistemic networks），提出分析机制：行动者通过破坏推理链条的可审计性来颠覆信任，并设计审计与补救基础设施。

Result: 识别出了一种新的信息操纵模式：攻击者不是直接提供虚假信息，而是利用支撑断言的推理链和制度认证的结构性漏洞，使原本可信的"支架化"传播变得不可审计，从而系统性破坏信任。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论为理解当代复杂传播环境中的信任破坏提供了更精确的分析工具，并指出了通过增强推理链的可审计性来修复信任的可行路径。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [62] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了医疗大语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展，提出了一种连接临床实践与计算方法的双重视角分析框架，涵盖从知识回忆到动态病例管理的五级能力体系，并在18个先进模型上进行了基准测试，揭示了专科模型在诊断任务中的优势与通用模型在决策支持中的表现，最后讨论了数据限制、幻觉和 grounding 等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域对LLMs的临床推理能力有迫切需求，但现有研究缺乏系统性地将临床实践能力层级与计算推理模式相连接的统一分析框架，亟需建立一套可评估和指导医疗LLM发展的结构化方法。

Method: 采用双视角分析方法：临床侧基于Miller金字塔建立从知识回忆到动态病例管理的五级能力方案；计算侧将演绎推理、归纳推理和溯因推理模式与常见医疗目标和任务相关联。同时构建覆盖五级医疗推理能力的基准数据集，对18个最先进模型进行评测。

Result: 医疗专科模型在诊断中心任务上表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话任务中领先。基准测试揭示了不同模型在不同推理层级上的能力差异。

Conclusion: 当前医疗LLMs取得显著进展但仍面临数据限制、幻觉和 grounding 问题等挑战，未来发展需要朝着更安全、更可靠、更契合临床工作流程的方向前进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [63] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐可信度"（alignment plausibility）概念，主张大型语言模型在心理健康领域的结构安全需要从价值规范、训练嵌入和部署监督三个层面构建，以类比人类临床实践的安全保障体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在心理健康支持中，开发者安全响应多为被动反应，主要关注可见的急性伤害，而忽视了依赖、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大等长期隐患。需要建立结构性的安全保障框架。

Method: 借鉴人类临床实践的安全保障模式，提出三层对齐框架：1）基于临床实践规范性承诺的显式价值规范；2）将这些价值嵌入模型的训练过程；3）部署中检测漂移和长期伤害的监督机制。并类比生物学可信度，提出"对齐可信度"作为监管概念。

Result: 构建了"对齐可信度"概念——一个系统在价值规范、训练机制和监督机制上共同与安全积极结果一致的架构性论证方式。

Conclusion: 对齐可信度可作为健康领域AI的监管概念，为判断系统是否真正对齐积极健康结果、即使在有能力造成伤害时也不会造成伤害、最终使患者受益提供原则性论证路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [64] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"idiobionics"这一新兴研究方向，系统研究智能仿生肢体的隐私风险与安全问题，通过展示潜在对抗攻击路径并梳理开放研究问题，旨在推动可穿戴机器人与人体增强设备的隐私保护研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能仿生肢体（如机器人假肢）集成先进传感器和AI控制技术，其能力大幅提升的同时也引入了新的隐私威胁向量，这些风险可能被恶意实体利用侵犯用户隐私，影响用户采纳意愿。因此需要系统性地理解和解决这些隐私问题。

Method: 定义"idiobionics"这一新研究领域，将其扎根于相关文献，通过展示和讨论潜在的对抗性攻击案例提供初步证据，并梳理出该领域内的开放研究问题清单。

Result: 提出了idiobionics的定义，展示了智能仿生肢体可能面临的对抗性攻击路径，整理了一份面向可穿戴机器人及其他面向人的自主系统研究者的开放研究问题清单。

Conclusion: idiobionics研究有助于释放机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的全部潜力，呼吁学界关注隐私与智能仿生肢体交叉领域的问题。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [65] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infinity-Parser2 是一个结合可控数据合成流水线与多任务强化学习的大规模多模态文档解析模型，通过构建500万样本的中英双语语料库 Infinity-Doc2-5M，在八个协同训练目标上联合优化，发布了推理优化版（Flash）和精度优先版（Pro），在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文档解析中忠实标注的解析语料长期稀缺的问题，现有方法受限于人工标注成本高、数据覆盖不足、模型泛化能力弱等瓶颈。

Method: 1）构建可控渲染框架与迭代精炼循环的可扩展合成引擎，生成多元文档类型的双语语料库；2）设计可验证的多任务奖励系统，在八个目标（文档解析、布局分析、表格解析、数学公式解析、图表解析、化学式解析、文档VQA、通用多模态理解）上联合强化学习；3）发布两个变体：Flash版优化低延迟推理，Pro版优化精度。

Result: Infinity-Parser2-Pro 在 olmOCR-Bench 上达 87.6%，ParseBench 上达 74.3%，超越 DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5、MinerU2.5；Flash 版吞吐量较 Infinity-Parser-7B 提升 3.68 倍，且在图表、化学式和文档 VQA 上展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过数据合成与多任务强化学习的有机结合，Infinity-Parser2 在文档解析精度与推理效率之间取得了有效平衡，验证了合成数据结合强化学习在全场景文档理解中的潜力。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [66] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VectorizationLLM是一个基于Google开源大语言模型的专用LLM，旨在辅助学生学习MATLAB中的向量化、时/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程。该模型通过RAG知识库和系统提示架构设计，提供概念讲解和课堂笔记示例，但不直接给出答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为CTEC 247课程（纽约理工学院电气与计算机工程技术系的应用计算分析II）的学生提供一个智能教学助手，帮助学生掌握MATLAB中复杂的计算分析方法，同时避免直接提供答案，促进自主学习。

Method: 基于Google开源大语言模型进行微调，采用RAG（检索增强生成）知识库和系统提示架构构建。模型响应中包含代码、文本和图像等多种形式的示例，且设计为仅提供概念讲解和课堂笔记示例，不直接给出问题答案。

Result: 构建了一个能够辅助学生学习向量化、时/波向量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等MATLAB概念的专用教学LLM。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM展示了专用大语言模型在工程教育领域作为教学辅助工具的潜力，通过RAG架构和系统提示设计，在提供指导性学习体验的同时避免直接答案，有助于培养学生的独立思考能力。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [67] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图网络的表面肌电信号(sEMG)表征方法，利用图神经网络实现实时手势识别。使用Myoband采集8名健康受试者的8通道sEMG信号，通过构建图网络捕捉前臂肌肉激活模式。该方法在M1 Pro CPU上平均分类准确率达99%，图构建与预测总耗时仅48ms，优于现有技术，适用于实时应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现先进假手和增强现实的无缝控制，需要准确、即时的手势识别。现有sEMG手势识别方法在实时性和准确性方面仍有提升空间，亟需一种既能捕捉肌肉激活空间结构信息，又满足实时性要求的新型表征与识别方法。

Method: 提出基于图网络的sEMG信号表征方法，构建包含前臂肌肉激活模式信息的图结构；在此基础上设计图神经网络(GNN)算法进行实时手势识别。使用Myoband（8电极环绕前臂）采集8名健康受试者的sEMG数据进行评估。

Result: 平均分类准确率达99%，超越当前最先进方法。在M1 Pro CPU上图构建与预测平均耗时48ms，满足实时应用需求。

Conclusion: 基于图网络的sEMG表征与图神经网络方法在高精度实时手势识别方面具有显著优势，为假肢控制和增强现实交互提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [68] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从传统规则自动化到LLM、RAG及多智能体“代理型”系统等AI架构如何支持精算实践中的透明度、可审计性和人机协同治理，并通过构建合成实验环境，对比了三种承保流程（单LLM基线、朴素RAG、多智能体Agentic RAG），发现Agentic RAG系统在多步骤决策和缺失信息场景下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正在重塑精算实践，尤其在需要处理非结构化文档、异构数据源和受监管决策流程的领域。精算师面临从传统规则自动化到大语言模型、RAG及多智能体系统等广泛的设计空间，需要研究这些新兴架构如何支持精算行业对透明度、可审计性和人机协同治理的核心需求，特别是直通式决策流程。

Method: 构建了合成但现实的实验环境，设计和实现了三种承保管线：(i)单LLM基线系统，(ii)朴素RAG系统，(iii)多智能体Agentic RAG系统（结合定向检索、第三方数据检查和显式多步规则评估），以小型商业企业主保单（BOPs）的直通式核保为具体应用场景进行对比分析。

Result: 多智能体Agentic RAG系统整体表现最优，尤其在多步骤决策和缺失信息场景中提升最为显著。结构化检索和反思机制帮助模型避免不合理的直通式决策。

Conclusion: 新兴AI架构能够有效支持精算实践中的监管和治理要求，其中多智能体代理型RAG系统通过结合定向检索、结构化规则评估和反思机制，在复杂决策场景下具有明显优势，可作为精算领域实现可控、可审计AI自动化的可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [69] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反馈操作正则化（FMR）方法，利用评估性反馈作为纠正信号改进模仿学习策略的对齐性，在安全健身房环境中实现高达98%的失调减少，且在数据稀缺场景下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法主要在多阶段流水线中结合人类演示和反馈，但缺乏在完全序列决策环境中利用这两种互补信号进行单阶段离线训练的研究。

Method: 提出反馈操作正则化（FMR），一种算法无关的方法，将评估性反馈作为纠正信号来提升模仿学习策略的对齐能力。通过改进安全健身房环境作为对齐评估的标准化测试平台。

Result: 在多种模仿学习算法上展示了对齐能力的提升，失调降低高达98%，并在有限数据（包括稀少的对齐样本和无信息噪声演示）条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FMR能够有效利用评估性反馈作为纠正信号，在序列决策环境中实现更好的对齐，且无需多阶段流水线，为单阶段离线对齐训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [70] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发布了尼日利亚工业机械数据集（89条记录，28个指标）和基于稀疏数值构建链式思维推理示例的方法，解决了语言模型构建数据集时提示与真实领域脱节的问题，使领域锚定提示从1/78提升至94/94，检索答案准确率达到84/84。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲经济体工业机械的公共、模型就绪数据稀缺，难以进行定量分析或训练语言模型处理数值任务。

Method: 1) 构建尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集（89条记录，28个指标，覆盖制造业和油气行业2006-2025年数据）；2) 提出从稀疏数值构建链式思维推理示例的方法，生成94组提示-补全-推理轨迹数据。

Result: 领域锚定提示从早期版本的1/78提升至94/94，检索答案匹配率84/84，所有数据均附有来源和代码簿。

Conclusion: 该数据集规模有限（89条记录，17个指标仅有一个观测值），主要作为参考和种子数据集，多数推理行为为检索而非多步计算。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [71] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用OCEAN人格框架（开放性、尽责性、外倾性、宜人性、神经质）对大规模语言模型的行为特征进行分解、测量和控制，通过训练低秩适配器实现人格特质的放大或抑制，并验证了这些特质轴对安全性行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具缺乏对LLM中反复出现的行为模式（即人格）进行可靠分解、测量和控制的手段，而这些人格模式影响模型的泛化能力和安全性。

Method: 采用OCEAN框架描述模型人格；训练低秩适配器（LoRA）放大或抑制个体特质；使用LLM评判器（经人工验证面板校准）、特质特异性多项选择题基准及标准能力评估来评估效果；引入无监督心理测量流程从模型生成中恢复可解释的行为因子。

Result: 在三个家族（4B-32B）的六个模型上，每个适配器以大致单调方式移动目标特质，与其他适配器近似可加性地组合以构建混合人格，且在中等规模下保持能力基准性能；沿神经质和宜人性轴移动分别影响挫败感和谄媚行为。

Conclusion: 人格控制可以通过在权重空间中学习、缩放和组合特质来实现，为人格测量、模型编辑和安全性之间搭建了桥梁。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [72] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了从视频中自动检测自闭症相关自我刺激行为的两大挑战：最优序列神经网络架构与时间采样率，以及小样本行为数据集的数据增强策略。LSTM和GRU模型在SSBD数据集上以每15帧采样达到97.5%和98.75%的峰值准确率，超越了此前CNN基线；水平翻转是最有效的单数据增强策略，而欠采样是复杂行为视频增强中不可或缺的技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过7500万自闭症谱系障碍患者缺乏可扩展的远程行为筛查计算方法，现有方法在自动化检测自闭症相关自我刺激行为方面存在架构选择、采样率确定和数据增强策略不明确的问题。

Method: 针对两个目标：(1) 在SSBD数据集上比较LSTM和GRU模型在1、5、15、30、45、90帧间隔下的性能；(2) 在I3D迁移学习流程中应用10种数据增强策略并通过消融研究量化每种技术的边际贡献，同时采用个性化机器学习方法对每个视频的时间分段进行训练和测试。

Result: LSTM和GRU在每15帧采样间隔下分别达到97.5%和98.75%的峰值准确率，优于此前CNN基线（62-76%）。水平翻转以48.78%的独立准确率最高，移除上采样导致最大性能下降。个性化模型预测一致（平均损失1.84，标准差0.79）。
